# fwid registry Enum capture - fwid_probe 1.4 (2026-10-16)
# HKLM\SYSTEM\CurrentControlSet\Enum\<root>: device-ID keys (depth 1) and instance keys (depth 2) as RegEnumKeyExW
# returns them to this user-mode process, with every value of each key (binary in full hex); subkeys of instance keys
# are listed by name only. KEY_READ only. Inaccessible keys are recorded ("open FAILED"), not counted as internal errors.
# The new VmLoader registry callback hides names containing VEN_15AD / VID_0E0F under Enum\PCI, \USB, \HDAUDIO from
# user-mode callers (vmloader_documentation.md section 3): compare the device-ID lists between runs and with enum_open_probe.txt.
# IDENTIFIERS: instance IDs, container IDs and USB serial numbers can be machine-unique: keep local.

########## Enum\PCI
